[EVENT: IRQ47369]
Date: 2023-07-02

Type: Protests > Peaceful protest | Disorder: Demonstrations
Actor1: Protesters (Iraq) [Protesters]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: ROJ News [Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
On 2 July 2023, locals from 11 villages blocked the Halabja - Sulaymaniyah road on the outskirts Saidsadiq town in Al Sulaymaniyah. The protesters demanded the road be repaired and denounced the lack of basic services. They also threatened to continue protesting until their demands are met. The protesters came from Biyarishk, Nughardan, Haji Qadir, Dulash, Shatwan, Mostakan, Kashdari, Qadir, and three other unspecified villages.

The warehouse owner was arrested.
